class="container relative z-10 "><div class="content"> <h2 class="text-lg md:text-xl" id="why-use-olbg-betting-tips">Why Use OLBG Betting Tips?</h2> <p>OLBG has been providing betting tips since 2002. Our tips come from a community of tipsters who take part in free-to-enter tipster competitions, which have paid out over £1,200,000 in prize money. The prizes give tipsters a reason to keep researching and sharing profitable selections across every sport we cover.</p><p>The competitions run separately for each sport, so our tips come from tipsters who specialise. We calculate the figures for you, and the profit stats shown for a sport only count tips placed in that sport. For example, the profit shown alongside football betting tips relates only to football tips.</p><p>Each selection shows how many tips it has received, the percentage of tips behind it and the tipsters' comments, so you can see the reasoning behind a prediction. Where tipster stats are shown, they include recent profit, profitable months and current form, such as profit this month or this week and days in profit.</p><p>To compare tipsters in more detail, see our <a href="/best-tipsters">best tipsters</a> pages. You can also browse all of today's tips in the <a href="/app">OLBG tips app</a>, which you can download from our site.</p> </div></div></section><section class="py-2 border-t lg:py-8 first:pt-0 first:mt-8 sct"><div class="container relative z-10 "><div class="content"> <h2 class="text-lg md:text-xl" id="where-can-i-find-a-more-detailed-explanation-of-a-betting-tip">Where Can I Find a More Detailed Explanation of a Betting Tip?</h2> <p>Many selections on this page come with comments from tipsters explaining the reasoning behind their tip, so you can decide which opinion you agree with. Reading them can point you towards bets you might not have considered.</p><p>If you prefer longer, article-style previews, our <a href="/blogs">blogs</a> section has previews of many popular events from OLBG experts, along with guidance on betting strategy.</p><h3>Where to Place Your Bets</h3><p>Once you have found the bets you want to place, the next step is choosing a bookmaker. OLBG has reviewed and rated UK-licensed <a href="/bookmakers.php">sports betting sites</a>, and our users add their own ratings and reviews.</p><p>For more detail, our <a href="/bookmakers/articles">bookmaker guides</a> include research on <a href="/bookmakers/articles/best-betting-apps">betting apps</a>, <a href="/bookmakers/articles/best-horse-racing">horse racing betting sites</a> and many other categories. If you are opening a new account, you can also check the latest <a href="/free.php">betting offers</a>.</p> </div></div></section><section class="py-2 border-t lg:py-8 first:pt-0 first:mt-8 sct"><div class="container relative z-10 "><div class="content"> <h2 class="text-lg md:text-xl" id="who-writes-and-maintains-this-page">Who Writes and Maintains This Page?</h2> <p>The guidance on this page is written by Darren and James and edited by Andy, who also keeps it up to date.</p><div class="author-box-component mb-8 p-6 bg-olbg-light-blue rounded-md items-center md:items-start justify-center md:justify-start flex flex-col md:flex-row color-bleu compact"><a class="rounded-full flex justify-center items-center overflow-hidden w-24 h-24 mb-6 md:mr-6 border-[6px] border-gray-500 border-opacity-25 image-outside" href="/meet-the-experts/darren-brett"><picture><source srcset="https://olbgimages.co.uk/image-cache?src=https://olbgimages.co.uk/storage/gallery/october-app/uploads/public/649/ec7/564/649ec7564fbfa346637961.jpeg&amp;w=80&amp;h=80&amp;zc=1" media="(max-width: 1023px)"> <source srcset="https://olbgimages.co.uk/image-cache?src=https://olbgimages.co.uk/storage/gallery/october-app/uploads/public/649/ec7/564/649ec7564fbfa346637961.jpeg&amp;w=100&amp;h=100&amp;zc=1" media="(min-width: 1024px)"> <img alt="Darren Brett" src="https://olbgimages.co.uk/image-cache?src=https://olbgimages.co.uk/storage/gallery/october-app/uploads/public/649/ec7/564/649ec7564fbfa346637961.jpeg&amp;w=80&amp;h=80&amp;zc=1" title="Darren Brett" class="w-full h-auto" width="80" height="80" loading="lazy"></picture></a> <div class="flex flex-col flex-1 items-center text-center md:items-start md:text-left"><p class="text-xl font-bold">About the author</p> <div class="mb-2">Darren has worked in the horse racing and gambling industries for over thirty years and has been writing about sport for most of that time. His specialist areas are National Hunt racing, snooker and golf.</div> <p class="mb-0 author-box-info">- <a class="font-bold underline text-olbg-blue-gray hover:text-olbg-orange" href="/meet-the-experts/darren-brett">Darren Brett</a>, Content Editor</p></div></div><div class="author-box-component mb-8 p-6 bg-olbg-light-blue rounded-md items-center md:items-start justify-center md:justify-start flex flex-col md:flex-row color-bleu compact"><a class="rounded-full flex justify-center items-center overflow-hidden w-24 h-24 mb-6 md:mr-6 border-[6px] border-gray-500 border-opacity-25 image-outside" href="/meet-the-experts/james-banting"><picture><source srcset="https://olbgimages.co.uk/image-cache?src=https://olbgimages.co.uk/storage/gallery/october-app/uploads/public/646/cad/0b9/646cad0b934d0036752766.jpg&amp;w=80&amp;h=80&amp;zc=1" media="(max-width: 1023px)"> <source srcset="https://olbgimages.co.uk/image-cache?src=https://olbgimages.co.uk/storage/gallery/october-app/uploads/public/646/cad/0b9/646cad0b934d0036752766.jpg&amp;w=100&amp;h=100&amp;zc=1" media="(min-width: 1024px)"> <img alt="James Banting" src="https://olbgimages.co.uk/image-cache?src=https://olbgimages.co.uk/storage/gallery/october-app/uploads/public/646/cad/0b9/646cad0b934d0036752766.jpg&amp;w=80&amp;h=80&amp;zc=1" title="James Banting" class="w-full h-auto" width="80" height="80" loading="lazy"></picture></a> <div class="flex flex-col flex-1 items-center text-center md:items-start md:text-left"><p class="text-xl font-bold">About the author</p> <div class="mb-2">James has over 20 years of experience in the sports betting and horse racing industry. He produces betting content on the major sporting events throughout the year, focusing on horse racing and tennis, and also contributes to our cricket and golf coverage.</div> <p class="mb-0 author-box-info">- <a class="font-bold underline text-olbg-blue-gray hover:text-olbg-orange" href="/meet-the-experts/james-banting">James Banting</a>, Content Editor</p></div></div><div class="author-box-component mb-8 p-6 bg-[#414a5e] text-white rounded-md items-center md:items-start justify-center md:justify-start flex flex-col md:flex-row color-dark compact"><a class="rounded-full flex justify-center items-center overflow-hidden w-24 h-24 mb-6 md:mr-6 border-[6px] border-gray-500 border-opacity-25 image-outside" href="/meet-the-experts/andy-powell"><picture><source srcset="https://olbgimages.co.uk/image-cache?src=https://olbgimages.co.uk/storage/gallery/october-app/uploads/public/646/cb8/b22/646cb8b22351f225446960.jpg&amp;w=80&amp;h=80&amp;zc=1" media="(max-width: 1023px)"> <source srcset="https://olbgimages.co.uk/image-cache?src=https://olbgimages.co.uk/storage/gallery/october-app/uploads/public/646/cb8/b22/646cb8b22351f225446960.jpg&amp;w=100&amp;h=100&amp;zc=1" media="(min-width: 1024px)"> <img alt="Andy Powell" src="https://olbgimages.co.uk/image-cache?src=https://olbgimages.co.uk/storage/gallery/october-app/uploads/public/646/cb8/b22/646cb8b22351f225446960.jpg&amp;w=80&amp;h=80&amp;zc=1" title="Andy Powell" class="w-full h-auto" width="80" height="80" loading="lazy"></picture></a> <div class="flex flex-col flex-1 items-center text-center md:items-start md:text-left"><p class="text-xl font-bold">About the editor</p> <div class="mb-2">Andy has contributed to OLBG since 2005. He has covered horse racing trends for several years, analysing stats to find betting angles in the big races in the UK and abroad, and manages many of our event previews. As editor, he oversees the content on this page and keeps it up to date.</div> <p class="mb-0 author-box-info">- <a class="font-bold underline text-olbg-blue-gray hover:text-olbg-orange" href="/meet-the-experts/andy-powell">Andy Powell</a>, Content Editor</p></div></div> </div></div></section><section class="py-2 border-t lg:py-8 first:pt-0 first:mt-8 sct"><div class="container relative z-10 "><div class="content"> <div><header class="sct-hdr "><h3 class="text-2xl"><i class="text-2xl i-ui-faq text-olbg-orange"></i> FAQ</h3> <p class="mb-2 text-gray-400 tagline">Betting Tips FAQs</p></header> <div class="faq"><div class="flex flex-col gap-0 sm:gap-3 sm:flex-row fq-cnt"><ul class="flex-1 [&amp;&amp;]:p-0 [&amp;&amp;]:m-0 h-rst-ls"><li data-faq-card="" class="group p-2 px-3 [&amp;&amp;]:mb-3 hover:bg-[#fff6e8] hover:cursor-pointer list-none bg-white border rounded border-c-border-2 shadow-sm faq-crd" data-num="1"><h4 class="p-0 m-0"><button type="button" data-faq-toggle="" aria-expanded="false" class="flex items-center w-full p-0 m-0 text text-left bg-transparent border-0 qa-title"><span class="flex-1 max-md:!text-md max-md:leading-relaxed max-md:pr-10 md:!text-base">How do I win a bet every time?</span> <i class="i-ui-dropdown-arrow text-olbg-orange group-[.card-opened]:-rotate-90 text-xs"></i></button></h4> <div class="hidden group-[.card-opened]:block pb-0 mt-2 mb-0 answer"><p>You can't. No tip or bet wins every time. Instead, focus on value: a selection offers value when the odds on offer are bigger than its true chance of winning would justify. If you find value consistently, the odds will work in your favour over time. The Value Rating shown next to each tip on this page compares a selection's odds with the percentage of tips it has received, which can help you spot possible value, but it is a guide rather than a guarantee.</p></div></li><li data-faq-card="" class="group p-2 px-3 [&amp;&amp;]:mb-3 hover:bg-[#fff6e8] hover:cursor-pointer list-none bg-white border rounded border-c-border-2 shadow-sm faq-crd" data-num="2"><h4 class="p-0 m-0"><button type="button" data-faq-toggle="" aria-expanded="false" class="flex items-center w-full p-0 m-0 text text-left bg-transparent border-0 qa-title"><span class="flex-1 max-md:!text-md max-md:leading-relaxed max-md:pr-10 md:!text-base">Which betting tips site is the best?</span> <i class="i-ui-dropdown-arrow text-olbg-orange group-[.card-opened]:-rotate-90 text-xs"></i></button></h4> <div class="hidden group-[.card-opened]:block pb-0 mt-2 mb-0 answer"><p>The right betting tips site depends on what you are looking for. OLBG's tips come from a community of thousands of tipsters covering 20+ sports, and many selections include comments explaining the reasoning behind the tip. Alongside each tip, you can see how many tips it has received, the number of comments, how many OLBG Experts have backed it and its Value Rating, so you can judge it for yourself before deciding whether to follow it.</p></div></li><li data-faq-card="" class="group p-2 px-3 [&amp;&amp;]:mb-3 hover:bg-[#fff6e8] hover:cursor-pointer list-none bg-white border rounded border-c-border-2 shadow-sm faq-crd" data-num="3"><h4 class="p-0 m-0"><button type="button" data-faq-toggle="" aria-expanded="false" class="flex items-center w-full p-0 m-0 text text-left bg-transparent border-0 qa-title"><span class="flex-1 max-md:!text-md max-md:leading-relaxed max-md:pr-10 md:!text-base">Are betting tips worth following?</span> <i class="i-ui-dropdown-arrow text-olbg-orange group-[.card-opened]:-rotate-90 text-xs"></i></button></h4> <div class="hidden group-[.card-opened]:block pb-0 mt-2 mb-0 answer"><p>Some are. Tips from the OLBG community come with information you can use to judge them, including the percentage of tips behind a selection, tipster comments explaining the reasoning and, where shown, the tipster's recent profit and profitable months. No tip guarantees a win, and past profit does not guarantee future returns, so only stake what you can afford to lose. To compare tipsters in more detail, see our <a href="/best-tipsters">best tipsters</a> pages.</p></div></li></ul><ul class="flex-1 [&amp;&amp;]:p-0 [&amp;&amp;]:m-0 h-rst-ls"><li data-faq-card="" class="group p-2 px-3 [&amp;&amp;]:mb-3 hover:bg-[#fff6e8] hover:cursor-pointer list-none bg-white border rounded border-c-border-2 shadow-sm faq-crd" data-num="1"><h4 class="p-0 m-0"><button type="button" data-faq-toggle="" aria-expanded="false" class="flex items-center w-full p-0 m-0 text text-left bg-transparent border-0 qa-title"><span class="flex-1 max-md:!text-md max-md:leading-relaxed max-md:pr-10 md:!text-base">What is the best bet of the day?</span> <i class="i-ui-dropdown-arrow text-olbg-orange group-[.card-opened]:-rotate-90 text-xs"></i></button></h4> <div class="hidden group-[.card-opened]:block pb-0 mt-2 mb-0 answer"><p>There is no single best bet of the day, as it depends on what you value. A good starting point is the selection at the top of this page, which by default is the one that has received the most tips from the OLBG community. You can also use Customise Tips to tailor the list to your own criteria, and check each selection's Value Rating and comments before you decide.</p></div></li><li data-faq-card="" class="group p-2 px-3 [&amp;&amp;]:mb-3 hover:bg-[#fff6e8] hover:cursor-pointer list-none bg-white border rounded border-c-border-2 shadow-sm faq-crd" data-num="2"><h4 class="p-0 m-0"><button type="button" data-faq-toggle="" aria-expanded="false" class="flex items-center w-full p-0 m-0 text text-left bg-transparent border-0 qa-title"><span class="flex-1 max-md:!text-md max-md:leading-relaxed max-md:pr-10 md:!text-base">How are the betting tips on this page ordered?</span> <i class="i-ui-dropdown-arrow text-olbg-orange group-[.card-opened]:-rotate-90 text-xs"></i></button></h4> <div class="hidden group-[.card-opened]:block pb-0 mt-2 mb-0 answer"><p>By default, tips are ordered by the number of tips each selection has received from the OLBG tipster community, so the most-tipped selections appear at the top. We call these our hot tips. Each row also shows the percentage of tips behind the selection, the number of comments and its Value Rating, and you can sort the list using the column headings.</p></div></li></ul></div></div></div> <div> </div>

    // Listen for messages from the Flourish embeds to resize the iframes
    window.addEventListener("message", function (event) {
        try {
            let message = JSON.parse(event.data);
            if (message.sender == "Flourish" && message.context == "iframe.resize") {
                let iframe = document.querySelector('iframe[src="' + message.src + '"]');
                if(iframe) {
                    iframe.style.height = message.height + "px";
                }
            }
        } catch (e) {
            return;
        }
    });

    // GA4: listen for newsletter_form_submit from embed iframe and send direct gtag event
    window.addEventListener("message", function (e) {
        if (e.data && e.data.event === "newsletter_form_submit") {
            console.log('newsletter_form_submit', e.data);
            try {
                if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.gtag) {
                    window.gtag('event', 'newsletter_form_submit', {
                        newsletter_list_id: e.data.newsletter_list_id,
                        newsletter_list_name: e.data.newsletter_list_name,
                        subscribe_form_type: e.data.subscribe_form_type,
                        email_hash: e.data.email_hash || ''
                    });
                } else {
                    console.error('GA4 gtag not available for newsletter_form_submit');
                }
                if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.olbgPostHogCapture) {
                    window.olbgPostHogCapture('olbg_newsletter_signup_submit', {
                        newsletter_list_id: e.data.newsletter_list_id,
                        newsletter_list_name: e.data.newsletter_list_name,
                        subscribe_form_type: e.data.subscribe_form_type,
                        email_hash: e.data.email_hash || ''
                    });
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Error sending GA4 event newsletter_form_submit', error);
            }
        }
    });

    /**
     * Lazy loading of js
    */
    var jsInjected = [];

    /**
     * Checks if an element is in viewport (even partially)
     *
     * @param element
     */
    function isElementInViewPort(el)
    {
        const bounding = el.getBoundingClientRect();
        return (0 < bounding.top && bounding.top < (window.innerHeight || document.documentElement.clientHeight)) ||
            (0 < bounding.bottom && bounding.bottom < (window.innerHeight || document.documentElement.clientHeight));
    }

    /**
         *
         * @param {*} path path to the script to lazy load
         * @param {*} element element to monitor for entering screen
         * @param {*} minTime force delay manually - to overcome situations where the element is in the first screen and we still want to delay its load
         */
    function asyncLoadJs(path, element, minTime)
    {

        /** We don't really want to check for any element, but just a delayed load */
        if (element === false && typeof minTime !== 'undefined' && minTime > 0) {

            window.addEventListener("load", function ()
            {
                setTimeout(function ()
                {
                    injectJs(path);
                }, minTime);
            }, false);

            return;
        }

        var elementToCheck = null;

        // Helper function to get the element
        function getElement()
        {
            if (!elementToCheck) {
                elementToCheck = document.querySelector(element);
            }
            return elementToCheck;
        }

        var listenerHandler = function ()
        {
            checkAndInject(path, getElement());
        };

        // Replace DOMContentLoaded with a more SvelteKit-friendly approach
        if (document.readyState === 'complete' || document.readyState === 'interactive') {
            setTimeout(() =>
            {
                elementToCheck = getElement();
                if (elementToCheck !== null) {
                    if (!checkAndInject(path, elementToCheck, minTime)) {
                        document.addEventListener('scroll', listenerHandler);
                    }
                }
            }, 0);
        } else {
            document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function ()
            {
                elementToCheck = getElement();
                if (elementToCheck !== null) {
                    if (!checkAndInject(path, elementToCheck, minTime)) {
                        document.addEventListener('scroll', listenerHandler);
                    }
                }
            });
        }

        /**
         * We also want to check in case the height of the document changes
         * (ie ads injected or ads removed based on geo)
         * Note: This part is commented out because it's not supported by IE11 :-         * */
        /*const resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(entries => {
            checkAndInject(path, element);
        });
        resizeObserver.observe(document.body);*/

        function checkAndInject(path, element, minTime)
        {

            if (isElementInViewPort(element) && !jsInjected.includes(path)) {

                if (typeof minTime !== 'undefined' && minTime > 0) {

                    setTimeout(function ()
                    {
                        injectJs(path);
                    }, minTime);

                } else {

                    injectJs(path);
                }

                document.removeEventListener('scroll', listenerHandler);
                return true;

            }

            return false;
        }

        function injectJs(path)
        {
            var s = document.createElement('script');
            s.type = 'text/javascript';
            s.async = true;
            s.src = path;
            var x = document.getElementsByTagName('script')[0];
            x.parentNode.insertBefore(s, x);
            jsInjected.push(path);
        }

    }

            (function initFaqCardToggles() {
                function bind() {
                    document.querySelectorAll('[data-faq-toggle]').forEach(function (el) {
                        if (el.getAttribute('data-faq-bound') === '1') return;
                        el.setAttribute('data-faq-bound', '1');
                        el.addEventListener('click', function () {
                            var card = el.closest('[data-faq-card]');
                            if (!card) return;
                            var opened = card.classList.toggle('card-opened');
                            el.setAttribute('aria-expanded', opened ? 'true' : 'false');
                        });
                    });
                }
                if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
                    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', bind);
                } else {
                    bind();
                }
            })();
